### Task 26: In-app updates (following OpenEnlarge)

**Files:**
- Modify: `app/src-tauri/Cargo.toml` (`tauri-plugin-updater`, `tauri-plugin-process`), `app/src-tauri/src/lib.rs` (plugins), `app/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json` (`plugins.updater`, dmg target), `app/src-tauri/capabilities/default.json`, `app/package.json`
- Create: `app/src/lib/update/updater.svelte.ts`, `app/src/lib/update/UpdatePrompt.svelte`, `.github/workflows/release.yml`
- Modify: `app/src/lib/components/SettingsSheet.svelte` (Check for updates), `app/src/routes/+page.svelte`, `app/src/lib/i18n/*.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: OpenEnlarge's updater (`~/Repos/filmrev/app/src/lib/update/updater.ts`, `UpdatePrompt.svelte`, `.github/workflows/release.yml`, its `plugins.updater` block and plugin registration) — reused and trimmed: `compareVersions`/`shouldPrompt` are dropped because the plugin's `check()` only returns releases newer than the running version (`tauri-plugin-updater` 2.9 `updater.rs`: `None => release.version > self.current_version`), so "not skipped" is the only check left; the last-check time and skipped version live in `localStorage` (a per-device convenience) instead of OpenEnlarge's catalog.
- Produces:
  - `updater.{state, autoCheck(), manualCheck(), install(), skip(), dismiss()}`; `UpdateState = idle | available {version, notes} | downloading {fraction} | upToDate | error`. `autoCheck` runs at launch at most once a day and stays silent on failure; `manualCheck` always checks and reports "up to date" or a failure; `install` downloads, installs and relaunches.
  - `UpdatePrompt` (a sheet: version, plain-text release notes, Install and restart / Later / Skip this version).
  - Settings' About row gains "Check for updates".
  - `tauri.conf.json` `plugins.updater` with **placeholders** `GITHUB-OWNER` (endpoint) and `REPLACE-WITH-UPDATER-PUBLIC-KEY` (pubkey). Both are filled in by the user from README.md "Before the first release" once the repo and signing key exist; nothing in this task makes or needs a key, and the app builds and runs with the placeholders (checks just fail quietly at launch).
  - `.github/workflows/release.yml`: on a `v*` tag, builds the Apple Silicon app with `tauri-action`, signs updater artifacts with the `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY` secrets, and attaches the `.dmg` and `latest.json` to a draft release.

- [ ] **Step 2: Updater state**

`app/src/lib/update/updater.svelte.ts`:

```ts
import { isTauri } from "@tauri-apps/api/core";
import { relaunch } from "@tauri-apps/plugin-process";
import { check, type Update } from "@tauri-apps/plugin-updater";

export type UpdateState =
  | { kind: "idle" }
  | { kind: "available"; version: string; notes: string }
  | { kind: "downloading"; fraction: number }
  | { kind: "upToDate" }
  | { kind: "error" };

const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;

function read(key: string): string {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(key) ?? "";
  } catch {
    return "";
  }
}

function write(key: string, value: string) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(key, value);
  } catch {
    return;
  }
}

class Updater {
  state = $state<UpdateState>({ kind: "idle" });
  private pending: Update | null = null;

  /** At launch: at most once a day, quietly, and never for a skipped version. */
  async autoCheck() {
    if (!isTauri() || Date.now() - Number(read("update.lastCheck") || 0) < DAY_MS) return;
    write("update.lastCheck", String(Date.now()));
    const update = await check().catch(() => null);
    if (update && update.version !== read("update.skip")) this.offer(update);
  }

  /** From Settings: always checks, and says when there is nothing new. */
  async manualCheck() {
    write("update.lastCheck", String(Date.now()));
    try {
      const update = await check();
      if (update) this.offer(update);
      else this.state = { kind: "upToDate" };
    } catch {
      this.state = { kind: "error" };
    }
  }

  /** Downloads and installs the offered update, then restarts the app. */
  async install() {
    if (!this.pending) return;
    this.state = { kind: "downloading", fraction: 0 };
    let total = 0;
    let got = 0;
    try {
      await this.pending.downloadAndInstall((ev) => {
        if (ev.event === "Started") total = ev.data.contentLength ?? 0;
        else if (ev.event === "Progress") {
          got += ev.data.chunkLength;
          this.state = { kind: "downloading", fraction: total ? got / total : 0 };
        }
      });
      await relaunch();
    } catch {
      this.state = { kind: "error" };
    }
  }

  skip() {
    if (this.state.kind === "available") write("update.skip", this.state.version);
    this.dismiss();
  }

  dismiss() {
    this.state = { kind: "idle" };
  }

  private offer(update: Update) {
    this.pending = update;
    this.state = { kind: "available", version: update.version, notes: update.body ?? "" };
  }
}

export const updater = new Updater();
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The update window**

`app/src/lib/update/UpdatePrompt.svelte`:

```svelte
<script lang="ts">
  import { updater } from "./updater.svelte";
  import { t } from "$lib/i18n/index.svelte";
  import Sheet from "$lib/components/Sheet.svelte";
  import ArrowsClockwiseIcon from "phosphor-svelte/lib/ArrowsClockwiseIcon";
  import DownloadSimpleIcon from "phosphor-svelte/lib/DownloadSimpleIcon";
  import ClockIcon from "phosphor-svelte/lib/ClockIcon";
  import ProhibitIcon from "phosphor-svelte/lib/ProhibitIcon";
  import CheckIcon from "phosphor-svelte/lib/CheckIcon";

  const s = $derived(updater.state);
</script>

{#if s.kind !== "idle"}
  <Sheet icon={ArrowsClockwiseIcon} title={t("update.title")} onClose={() => updater.dismiss()}>
    {#if s.kind === "available"}
      <p class="muted">{t("update.available", { version: s.version })}</p>
      {#if s.notes}<pre class="notes">{s.notes}</pre>{/if}
      <div class="sfoot">
        <button class="btn ghost" onclick={() => updater.skip()}><ProhibitIcon />{t("update.skip")}</button>
        <button class="btn" onclick={() => updater.dismiss()}><ClockIcon />{t("update.later")}</button>
        <button class="btn accent" onclick={() => updater.install()}><DownloadSimpleIcon />{t("update.install")}</button>
      </div>
    {:else if s.kind === "downloading"}
      <p class="muted">{t("update.downloading")}</p>
      <div class="meter"><i style:width="{Math.round(s.fraction * 100)}%"></i></div>
    {:else}
      <p class="muted">{t(s.kind === "upToDate" ? "update.upToDate" : "update.failed")}</p>
      <div class="sfoot"><button class="btn accent" onclick={() => updater.dismiss()}><CheckIcon />{t("update.ok")}</button></div>
    {/if}
  </Sheet>
{/if}

<style>
  .notes { max-height: 200px; overflow: auto; white-space: pre-wrap; margin: var(--s3) 0 0; padding: var(--s3); border-radius: var(--r-sm); background: var(--bg); border: 1px solid var(--line); font: 12.5px/1.5 var(--body); }
  .meter { height: 8px; margin-top: var(--s3); border-radius: 4px; background: var(--raised); overflow: hidden; }
  .meter i { display: block; height: 100%; border-radius: 4px; background: var(--accent); transition: width var(--t) var(--ease); }
  .sfoot { display: flex; justify-content: flex-end; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: var(--s2); margin-top: var(--s5); }
</style>
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Release workflow**

`.github/workflows/release.yml`:

```yaml
name: Release

on:
  push:
    tags: ['v*']
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: write

jobs:
  build:
    runs-on: macos-14
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: dtolnay/rust-toolchain@stable
        with:
          targets: aarch64-apple-darwin

      - uses: swatinem/rust-cache@v2

      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: 20
          cache: npm
          cache-dependency-path: app/package-lock.json

      - name: Install frontend dependencies
        run: npm ci
        working-directory: app

      - uses: tauri-apps/tauri-action@v0
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
          APPLE_CERTIFICATE: ${{ secrets.APPLE_CERTIFICATE }}
          APPLE_CERTIFICATE_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.APPLE_CERTIFICATE_PASSWORD }}
          APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY }}
          APPLE_ID: ${{ secrets.APPLE_ID }}
          APPLE_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.APPLE_PASSWORD }}
          APPLE_TEAM_ID: ${{ secrets.APPLE_TEAM_ID }}
          TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY }}
          TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD }}
        with:
          projectPath: app
          tagName: ${{ github.ref_name }}
          releaseName: 'KaraAlwaysOK ${{ github.ref_name }}'
          releaseBody: 'Download the .dmg below. See the README for install notes.'
          releaseDraft: true
          prerelease: false
          args: '--target aarch64-apple-darwin --config {"bundle":{"createUpdaterArtifacts":true}}'
```

The app's endpoint (`releases/latest/download/latest.json`) resolves once a release is published, so review the draft and publish it.

### Task 27: README, full check, and the user's acceptance checklist

**Files:**
- Modify: `README.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything above.
- Produces: README sections for running the app; the app left running; the user acceptance checklist below.

- [ ] **Step 1: README**

In `README.md`, replace the paragraph starting "This repo is the engine so far" with:

```markdown
The repo holds the engine — a Rust library (`kara-core`) and a CLI (`kara-cli`) for adding
songs, preparing them (download, convert, look up lyrics, take the vocals out) and exporting
the result — and the desktop app in `app/` (Tauri 2 + Svelte 5), which plays prepared songs
live with the singer slider, key and synced lyrics. The app speaks English, Japanese, Korean,
Simplified Chinese, Traditional Chinese and Spanish, following the system language.
```

and add after the "Build" section (the "Development" section already says tests need `ffmpeg` and the app never does, which covers Task 3's cover-art test):

~~~markdown
## Run the app

```
cd app
npm install
npm run tauri:dev
```

`tauri:dev` hot-reloads the interface only; after changing Rust code, stop it and run it again.
The first launch downloads the singing engine (about 90 MB). The app and the CLI share the
same library in `~/Library/Application Support/kara-always-oki`, and only one of them can
use it at a time.
~~~

In "Before the first release", step 4 becomes:

```markdown
4. Put the public key in `plugins.updater.pubkey` in `app/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json`
   (replacing `REPLACE-WITH-UPDATER-PUBLIC-KEY`), and the repo owner in the updater
   endpoint URL (replacing `GITHUB-OWNER`).
5. Push a `v*` tag. The Release workflow builds a draft release with the `.dmg` and
   `latest.json`; publish it so the app's update check can find it.
```

```bash
source "$HOME/.cargo/env" && cargo test --workspace && cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings
cd app && npm test && npm run check
```
Expected: all Rust and vitest tests pass; 0 clippy warnings; 0 svelte-check errors.

- [ ] **Step 3: Leave the app running**

Run `source "$HOME/.cargo/env" && cargo build -p kara-app && cd app && npm run app-check -- ../.superpowers/sdd/2026-09-27-phase1b-app/shots/task-27.png` (it must pass, and its picture shows the library with the test song), then start `cd app && npm run tauri:dev` in the background and leave it open for the user with the checklist below.

- [ ] **Step 4: Commit**

```bash
git add README.md
git commit -m "docs: run the app; release checklist points at the updater placeholders"
```

## User acceptance checklist (for the user; does not block any task)

Hand this list to the user with the app running from `npm run tauri:dev`. Use `~/Music/To Infinity N Beyond.m4a`; make variants from a scratch copy, never the original.

1. **First run.** Quit the app, move `runtime/` and `models/` out of `~/Library/Application Support/kara-always-oki` into a scratch folder, relaunch: "Getting ready" fills with "N of M MB", then "All set", then the library. (Or skip: move nothing.)
2. **Browse.** Playlists › Imported lists the song; the Albums and Artists tabs list its album and artist.
3. **Library in use.** Quit the app, add a new song with the CLI and start preparing it (`target/release/kara add <file>`, then `target/release/kara prepare <its id>`, after `cargo build -p kara-cli --release`), and launch the app while it runs: the setup screen says another copy is using the library, with Try again. After the CLI finishes, Try again opens the library.
4. **Add a file.** Drop the song on the window: the dashed "Drop to sing" overlay; nothing plays or blocks; the toast steps reading → preparing the audio → finding the lyrics → "Added “…” to your library" with the album art; its row sits at the top of Imported, dimmed with a spinner while adding, and can't be queued from the row or its menu until done. Tapping the toast opens Imported with the row highlighted. Drop the same file again: "“…” is already in your library", and tapping it reveals the row.
5. **Sing it.** Tap the row: the row's thumbnail shows the waveform, the glass player bar shows the song with its progress line and singer slider, and the dark karaoke view opens (dark even in light mode, title top-left, collapse button, lyrics centered or "No lyrics found, sing it your way."), "Starting in Ns" counts down, singing starts within about 10 s. Lyrics (if found) are centered and fill word by word in time; three dots count down before the first line and before lines that follow a long instrumental break. The background drifts in the artwork's colors.
6. **Mix, key, timing.** Singer slider: fully left = the original with the singer, fully right = singer removed, in between mixes — live, no restart. "…" › Key +2 shifts the pitch, lyrics stay in time; back to 0 is the untouched original. Lyrics timing ±0.1 s moves the highlight. After a relaunch the slider and key are remembered for that song. If the key shifter can't start on this Mac, the Key row is hidden instead.
7. **Seams and seeking.** Listen across 0:10, 0:20, 0:30: no gaps or clicks. While a new song is still being prepared, click far right on the progress line: "That part isn't ready yet", and it lands just inside the lighter band. No "Catching up…" during normal play.
8. **Queue.** With one song playing, the queue panel shows Now playing and "Nothing up next". Tap two more songs and use Play next on one: the toasts say so; the queue panel shows the order; drag to reorder (the accent line shows where the row will land: above the target when dragging up, below it when dragging down), remove one; Next and Previous work; when the queue ends the bar keeps the last song.
9. **Links.** Paste a YouTube link of a song: skeleton, then thumbnail, title, channel, duration; press Enter: the toast uses the video's title, downloading → preparing → finding the lyrics → added, with the real thumbnail. Paste link A then link B quickly: A's preview never shows under B. Paste a new link and press Enter at once, before its preview shows: the toast says "Song from youtu.be" (in your language) until the real title arrives. A Spotify link shows the refusal hint. "zzqx" shows the nothing-matches message.
10. **Playlists.** Song menu › Add to playlist › New playlist…: the new playlist's name is selected in its sidebar row; type and press Enter: "Added to “…”". Make another from the sidebar's New playlist and press Esc: it disappears. The song menu check-marks playlists that already have the song. Reorder rows by dragging, with the line on the landing side (if dragging doesn't work in the window, report it: Task 24 describes the fallback). Remove from this playlist and Delete playlist both offer Undo; after the toast runs out they are gone and the songs stay in the library.
11. **Edit and delete.** Edit info changes title/artist/album, and the song moves to its new album/artist; the picture can't be edited. Delete from library → Undo works; after the toast runs out the song is gone and Settings shows less storage used. Deleting the playing song moves on to the next one.
12. **Already instrumental.** `ffmpeg -i "To Infinity N Beyond.m4a" -c copy -metadata title="To Infinity N Beyond (Instrumental)" inst.m4a`; drop `inst.m4a`: its singer slider starts fully left (original), and sliding it up still removes the singer.
13. **Duets.** `ffmpeg -i "To Infinity N Beyond.m4a" -c copy -metadata lyrics="$(printf '[00:05.00]M: Paper boats along the gutter\n[00:09.00]F: I folded mine from bus tickets\n[00:13.00]Both: we sail them anyway\n')" duet.m4a`; drop and sing `duet.m4a`: lines alternate left, right (accent tint) and centered.
14. **Settings.** Open with the gear: Storage used/free, the limit stepper, Clear storage (disabled while a song is being added; the playing song keeps playing). Language: all six, labels change at once, nothing overflows, CJK in the system fonts, song titles and lyrics unchanged. About row: icon, KaraAlwaysOK, Version 0.1.0; Check for updates closes Settings and shows the update window with "Couldn't check for updates. Try again later." until README "Before the first release" is done.
15. **Clean quit.** Quit with ⌘Q: the terminal running `tauri:dev` shows no CoreML or ONNX text after the window closes.
16. **System settings, live.** Light/Dark follows macOS while running; the karaoke view stays dark. Reduce motion: slides become fades and the background stops. Reduce transparency (then click into the app): glass surfaces turn solid.
17. **Errors in your language.** With Settings on 日本語, drop a text file renamed to `.mp3`: the message is in Japanese. Move a library song's file away and play it: the "moved or deleted" message is in Japanese with Try again.
